Chapter VI · Unit 132

The analysis at the real places: tower measures, gauges, open-cell charts and Plancherel bounds

note and structured proofs · 239 declarations · 108 structured proofs · 3 notes

Unit 132 (New/RealPlace) is the analysis at the real places that Theorem B (enough forms) needs. Its first chunk (132-a) sets up the tower method: sizes and gauges (filtrations B_0 \subset B_1 \subset \cdots of the ring that bound the entries of group elements near a frame), a level (measures \theta_l on the floors of the unitary group of a split hermitian space, whose convolution is a Haar measure), pairing kernels averaged floor by floor, and the weight, fibre and Plancherel bounds on each floor; at the complex place it builds the level and the gauge explicitly, proves that the tower measure is a constant multiple of Haar measure with the constant controlled through an open-cell double coset chart (Jacobian and injectivity), and bounds integrals of Bessel functions over that chart. The second chunk (132-b) proves from these tools the local theorem (for a general place, then summed over shells at \mathbb{C}), the band limits, and the open-cell, cut-off and volume-kernel bounds.

Notes

  • The tower method at a place: floor measures, gauges and the bounds on each floor 27 pages · PDF
  • The local theorem for floor forms and the band limits at the complex place 28 pages · PDF
  • The complex place: the open-cell chart, the Haar measure of the tower, the Bessel cut and the volume weight 26 pages · PDF

Let \mathfrak{s} be a shape, T an \mathfrak{s}-form over A whose Gram matrix J_{T} = splitGram( T ) of V is hermitian and invertible (IsHermUnit), \iota a finite type and m \in \mathbb{N}; G_{T} = U ( J_{T} ), H_{l} = GT ( l ) (fixing e_{a} , e_{a}^{*} for a < l), Q_{l} = unitriGroup( l ), \mathcal{N}_{< l} = unipotentBelow( l ), f_{b} = besselFrame( b ) the frame (f_{b} = e_{b} for b < \varrho, f_{\varrho} = z). Unit 112 · Matrix determinant estimates and simultaneous eigenvectors · 19 declarations

  • Shape unit 112

    A shape: a natural number ϱ (the number of hyperbolic planes), a finite type κ (the index set of the basis of X₀; k = |κ|) and an element k₀ ∈ κ. The whole space V has basis e_0, …, e_{ϱ−1}, (x_i)_{i∈κ}, z, e*_{ϱ−1}, …, e*_0; r = 2ϱ + k + 1 is its rank and n = ϱ + 1 the length of the frame f = (e_0, …, e_{ϱ−1}, z).

  • Shape.Form.splitGram unit 112

    The Gram matrix J of V: the hyperbolic pairs (e_a, e*_a) with J(e_a, e*_a) = J(e*_a, e_a) = 1, orthogonal to each other and to V_ϱ = X₀ ⊕ Az, on which J is the form T.

  • Shape.Form.G unit 112

    The unitary group U(J) of V: invertible g with gᴴJg = J.

  • FI.level unit 112

    The level of a basis index: a for e_a, ϱ for the middle block (x_i and z), and 2ϱ − a for e*_a.

  • det_succ_row_zero' unit 112

    Laplace expansion along the first row: for a square matrix M of size j+1, det M = Σ_c (−1)^c M_{0c} det(M with row 0 and column c removed).

  • det_eq_one_of_unitriangular unit 112

    A square matrix Z that agrees with the identity at every entry (a, b) with lev(b) ≤ lev(a), for some level function lev, has determinant 1.

  • frameMatrix unit 112

    frameMatrix(γ): the block of γ on the frame rows and columns.

  • Shape.Form.besselU0 unit 112

    𝖴⁰: the elements of 𝖴 that fix every vector of the frame.

  • Shape.Form.besselU unit 112

    𝖴: the elements of U(J) that are block upper triangular for the levels, with identity diagonal blocks.

  • norm_det_le_norm_pow unit 112

    |det x| ≤ ‖x‖^n for an n×n matrix x.

  • leadingMinor unit 112

    D_l(Z): the determinant of the upper-left l×l block of Z.

  • adjugate_fin_succ_eq_det_submatrix' unit 112

    Cofactor formula for the adjugate of a square matrix of size ϱ + 1.

  • HCParam.psiInFrame unit 112

    psiInFrame(l, g) = ψ_λ(p⁻¹gp) on U(J), for l = 2λ and the frame p.

  • frameEquiv unit 112

    frameEquiv: U(J) → U(diag ε), g ↦ p⁻¹gp (an isomorphism of topological groups).

  • isIdempotentKernel_psiInFrame unit 112

    g ↦ deg_λ·ψ_λ(p⁻¹gp) is an idempotent kernel on U(J) (gap 500n³ + A). The second reader: formal-degree orthogonality (Harish-Chandra) accounts for the integral identity; the explicit integrability threshold and the normalisation are the artifact's own (literature: unresolved).

  • colLattice unit 112

    colLattice(R, M): the R-module spanned by the columns of M.

  • preimageLattice unit 112

    preimageLattice(R, M): the vectors v with Mv integral.

  • hua_integral_le unit 112

    Hua's integral bound: ∫ c·|det(M + Σ_{i∈s} y_iᴴy_i)|^{−(|s|+r)} over rows x_i ∈ ℂ^k (y_i = x_iS + a_i) is at most c·(π^k/(k!|det S|²))^{|s|}·|det M|^{−r}, M positive definite, det S ≠ 0, r ≥ k. The second reader: an upper bound that follows from Hua's exact iterated beta integral, with translations and a linear change in each row (literature: weakens).

  • posSemidef_bessel unit 112

    Bessel's inequality: if ‖E‖ ≤ 1 then X(1 − EEᴴ)Xᴴ is positive semidefinite.

Unit 119 · Flag stabilizers and the open Bruhat cell of a unitary group · 99 declarations

  • weylElem unit 119

    For l ∈ ℕ and units p : ℕ → Aˣ, the Weyl element w_l(p) ∈ U(J): e_a ↦ p_a e*_a and e*_a ↦ (p_a*)⁻¹ e_a for the pairs a < l (a < ϱ), fixing the other basis vectors.

  • floorOf unit 119

    The floor of a basis index: a for e_a and e*_a, ϱ for z, and ϱ + 1 = n for the basis vectors x_i of X₀.

  • Shape.Form.unitriGroup unit 119

    Q_l (l ≤ ϱ + 1): the elements of U(J) whose matrix agrees with the identity at every entry (i, j) with level(j) ≤ level(i) and max(floorOf(j), level(j)) < l (unitriangular on the first l vectors of the frame); for l > ϱ + 1 the condition holds for all such entries (Q_l = 𝖴).

  • Shape.Form.unipotentBelow unit 119

    𝒩_{<l}: the elements of U(J) that are block upper triangular with identity diagonal blocks for the levels lev(i) = level(i) if floorOf(i) < l and ϱ otherwise (the flag e_0, …, e_{l−1}, V_l, e*_{l−1}, …, e*_0); for l ≥ ϱ it is 𝖴.

  • unitriGroup_dual_row unit 119

    For s ∈ Q_j and a pair index a < j: the row of s at e*_a agrees with the identity matrix at every column k other than the e*_b with b < a.

  • unitriGroup_mulVec_dual unit 119

    For s ∈ Q_j, a pair index a < j and a vector v with v(e*_b) = 0 for all b < a: (sv)(e*_a) = v(e*_a).

  • unipotentBelow_le_unitriGroup unit 119

    𝒩_{<j} ⊂ Q_l for all j, l.

  • mem_unipotentBelow unit 119

    g ∈ 𝒩_{<l} if and only if g_{ij} = δ_{ij} whenever lev(j) ≤ lev(i), for lev(i) = level(i) if floorOf(i) < l and ϱ otherwise.

  • splitGram_eq_ite_oppositeIndex unit 119

    If floorOf(i) < ϱ or floorOf(j) < ϱ, then J_{ij} = 1 if j is the opposite index of i (e_a ↔ e*_a) and 0 otherwise.

  • level_le_or_exists_dual unit 119

    Every basis index k has level(k) ≤ ϱ or is e*_b with floorOf(k) = b.

  • HyperbolicBasis unit 119

    A hyperbolic basis of V_ϱ = X₀ ⊕ Az: a matrix E (columns: one hyperbolic plane, m further hyperbolic pairs and an orthonormal part indexed by ι) with EᴴTE = planeSumGram(hyperbolic Gram matrix), k + 1 = |ι| + 2m + 2, and md(det T) = 1.

  • HyperbolicBasis.floorQuadric unit 119

    For a hyperbolic basis E and b ≤ ϱ (and a skew β, by default δ·J(f_b, f_b)), floorQuadric(E, b, β) = δ^(b): the flat-chart quadric measure on the quadric h(v, v) = h(f_b, f_b) of V_b, carried to V by the basis of V_b made of E and the pairs (e_a, e*_a), a > b.

  • Place.weylLaw unit 119

  • Place.heisenbergMeasure unit 119

  • descConv unit 119

    For J = 1, det(1 − ½·skewOfReal(y)) ≠ 0 for every real y.

  • skewCoords unit 115

    The real-linear map sending a complex matrix x to the real matrix with entries (Re x_ab + Im x_ab), divided by √2 off the diagonal; a left inverse of skewOfReal.

  • skewOfReal unit 115

    The real-linear map sending a real matrix y to the skew-hermitian matrix with entries i·y_aa on the diagonal and ((1+i)y_ab − (1−i)y_ba)/√2 off it.

  • Haar.lintegral_chart_rotate_le unit 115

    For J a hermitian involution and Φ : V → U(J) injective and differentiable on a measurable A with Jacobian w for Jg⁻¹dg in measure-preserving coordinates e: for almost every real t and all f ≥ 0, ∫_A (w(p)/vol U(n)) f(e^{it}Φ(p)) dp ≤ ∫ f dHaar.

  • lintegral_cayleyDensity_skewColumnEquiv unit 115

    Integrating cayleyDensity(1, skewColumnEquiv(k₀)(θ, η)) over θ gives 2πⁿ/(n−1)! times cayleyDensity(1, η) (both sides divided by a positive constant).

  • lintegral_eq_cayleyChart unit 115

    The Haar integral over U(J) in Cayley coordinates: ∫ f = ∫_{det(cayleyDenom) ≠ 0} (cayleyDensity(J, y)/vol U(n)) f(cayleyChart(J, y)) dy.

  • circleScalar unit 115

    For real t, the scalar e^{it}·1 as an element of U(J).

  • volU unit 115

    vol U(j) = ∏_{i<j} 2π^{i+1}/i!.

  • HasGaussianIntegral.measurePreserving unit 115

    A real-linear map that preserves the Gaussian quadratic forms is measure preserving (Lebesgue measures normalised by the Gaussian integral).

  • cayleyDenom unit 115

    cayleyDenom(J, y) = 1 − ½J⁻¹·skewOfReal(y), the denominator of Cayley's chart.

  • measure_sublevel_ne_zero unit 115

    For an open-positive measure and continuous f with f(1) < r, the set {f ≤ r} has nonzero measure.

    ∫ (2/‖j(s)y‖)^c dν(s) ≤ 16^c·ν{s : ‖j(s) − 1‖ ≤ 1}.

Unit 10 · Lattice fundamental domains and holomorphic parameter integrals · 5 declarations

  • norm_sub_le_of_holomorphic_bdd unit 10

    Schwarz lemma in Lipschitz form: if f (between complex normed spaces) is complex differentiable with ‖f‖ ≤ B on the open ball of radius r about c, then ‖f(z) − f(c)‖ ≤ (2B/r)‖z − c‖ for z in that ball. The second reader reads it as a consequence of the scalar Schwarz lemma on the complex line through c and z, without its derivative estimate or equality case (literature: weakens).

  • l2_opNorm_le_of_apply unit 10

    If every entry of an m×n matrix A has |A_ij| ≤ M, then ‖A‖ ≤ mnM.

  • norm_apply_le_l2_opNorm unit 10

    |A_ij| ≤ ‖A‖.

  • sobolev_chain unit 10

    Sobolev chain inequality: for measurable f_i(x, ω), i words in L, with ‖∂_d f_i‖ ≤ Λ Σ_l ‖f_{l::i}‖ on the cube [0, ε]^D (0 < ε ≤ 1) for almost all ω, and ∬‖f_i‖² ≤ K on the cube for |i| ≤ |D|: ∫‖f_∅(0, ω)‖² dω ≤ (2(1 + (|L|Λ)²)/ε)^{|D|}·K.

  • ostrowski_taussky unit 10

    Ostrowski–Taussky: H ⪰ 0 and Θ + Θᴴ − H ⪰ 0 imply |det H| ≤ 2^n|det Θ|.

Unit 114 · Iwasawa integration and means on compact quotients · 4 declarations

  • identDistrib_orbitMeasure_comp unit 114

    For measure-preserving σ : H → H' and T : X → Y with T(hx) = σ(h)T(x), and an H'-invariant F, F ∘ T and F are identically distributed for the quotient measures.

  • lintegral_orbitMeasure_lintegral unit 114

    Unfolding: let H act measurably on X with a cutoff β, ν be an s-finite inversion-invariant measure on H and μ an s-finite H-invariant measure on X. For measurable f : X → [0, ∞]: ∫_X (∫_H f(hx) dν(h)) d(orbitMeasure ν μ)(x) = ∫_X f dμ, where orbitMeasure ν μ = β·μ is the quotient measure on H∖X realised on X through the cutoff (∫_H β(hx) dν(h) = 1); infinite values are allowed. The authors cite Weil's quotient-integration formula; the second reader finds the statement more general than the cited proper Haar-action setting (literature: unresolved).

  • exists_eq_smul_haar unit 114

    On a second countable locally compact group with a Haar measure μ that is also right invariant, every s-finite right-invariant measure ν is d·μ for some d ∈ [0, ∞] (d = 0 and d = ∞ allowed). The second reader: an extension of the uniqueness of regular Haar measures to s-finite measures, with an infinite multiplier, derivable from s-finite Tonelli (literature: unresolved).

  • identDistrib_orbitMeasure_of_unfold unit 114

    Unfolding as identical distribution: if (z, l) ↦ σ(l)·T(z) carries m × ρ to μ, then F under orbitMeasure(ν, μ) and F ∘ T under m are identically distributed, for measurable H-invariant F.
